\phi=\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{s^{\prime}}}}{[C\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\lozenge}\nolimits_{\mathsf{s}}}D]}

Assume
\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{s^{\prime}}}}[C\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\lozenge}\nolimits_{\mathsf{s}}}D]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}

Base Case

: First, let us show that
\mathfrak{D}_{0}\models\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{s^{\prime}}}}[C\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\lozenge}\nolimits_{\mathsf{s}}}D]
. Let
\pi^{\prime}\in\sigma_{0}(\hbox{$\mathsf{s^{\prime}}$})
and
C\in\Lambda_{\pi^{\prime}}(\delta)
.

(1)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi^{\prime}}$}}}}[C\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\lozenge}\nolimits_{\mathsf{s}}}D]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}
by assumption and
Lemma

9

(2)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{u}}}[\{a\}\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi^{\prime}}$}}}}[\top\Rightarrow C]]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
, by construction and
Lemma

9

(3)

\mathsf{s}[a,D]\prec\mathsf{s}
by construction

(4)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{*}}}[\{a\}\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{s}}}[D\Rightarrow P_{\mathsf{s},a,D}]]
by construction

(5)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{*}}}[P_{\mathsf{s},a,D}\sqsubseteq{\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{s}}}}_{[a,D]}[\top\Rightarrow D]]
by construction

(6)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{*}}}[\{a\}\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi^{\prime}}$}}}}[\top\Rightarrow C]]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
, by (2) and rule A.3.

(7)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi^{\prime}}$}}}}[\{a\}\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi^{\prime}}$}}}}[\top\Rightarrow C]]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
, by (6) and rule S.3

(8)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi^{\prime}}$}}}}[\top\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi^{\prime}}$}}}}[\{a\}\Rightarrow C]]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
, by (7) and rule I.1

(9)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi^{\prime}}$}}}}[\{a\}\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\lozenge}\nolimits_{\mathsf{s}}}D]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}
by (8) and rule C.3

(10)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi^{\prime}}$}}}}[\{a\}\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{s}}}[D\Rightarrow P_{\mathsf{s},a,D}]]
by (4) and rule S.3

(11)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi^{\prime}}$}}}}[\{a\}\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\lozenge}\nolimits_{\mathsf{s}}}P_{\mathsf{s},a,D}]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}
by (10) and rule C.4

(12)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{s}}}[P_{\mathsf{s},a,D}\sqsubseteq{\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{s}}}}_{[a,D]}[\top\Rightarrow D]]
by (5) and rule S.3

(13)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi^{\prime}}$}}}}[\{a\}\sqsubseteq{\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{s}}}}_{[a,D]}[\top\Rightarrow D]]
by (11), (12) and rule F.3, as desired

From this we obtain that if
\pi^{\prime}\in\sigma_{0}(\hbox{$\mathsf{s^{\prime}}$})
and
C\in\Lambda_{\pi^{\prime}}(\delta)
, then
D\in\Lambda_{\pi}(\delta)
for
\pi\in\sigma_{0}(\mathsf{s}[a,D])
and hence
\pi\in\sigma_{0}(\hbox{$\mathsf{s}$})
, thus
\mathfrak{D}_{0},\pi\models C\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\lozenge}\nolimits_{\mathsf{s}}}D
for all
\pi\in\sigma_{0}(\hbox{$\mathsf{s^{\prime}}$})
and hence
\mathfrak{D}_{0}\models\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{s^{\prime}}}}[C\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\lozenge}\nolimits_{\mathsf{s}}}D]
by the semantics.

Inductive Step

: Now, we assume that
\mathfrak{D}_{i}\models\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{s^{\prime}}}}[C\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\lozenge}\nolimits_{\mathsf{s}}}D]
and show that
\mathfrak{D}_{i+1}\models\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{s^{\prime}}}}[C\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\lozenge}\nolimits_{\mathsf{s}}}D]
. Consider
\delta\in\Delta_{i+1}\setminus\Delta_{i}
(the case of
\delta\in\Delta_{i}
is trivial). Thus,
\delta
has been introduced to satisfy some axiom
\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{t}}}[E\sqsubseteq\exists R.F]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
. We must show that if
C\in\Lambda_{\pi^{\prime}}(\delta)
then
D\in\Lambda_{\pi}(\delta)
for some
\pi\in\sigma_{i+1}(\hbox{$\mathsf{s}$})
, specifically for
\pi=\pi_{\delta^{\prime},\mathord{\mathop{\lozenge}\nolimits_{\mathsf{s}}}D}
. We consider the three cases.

Case 1

: Assume
\pi^{\prime}=\pi^{*}
,
\pi\in\sigma_{i+1}(\hbox{$\mathsf{s^{\prime}}$})
and
C\in\Lambda_{\pi^{\prime}}(\delta)
.

(1)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{u}}}[\top\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi^{\prime}}$}}}}[F\Rightarrow C]]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
from
C\in\Lambda_{\pi}(\delta)
and
Lemma

9

(2)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi^{\prime}}$}}}}[C\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\lozenge}\nolimits_{\mathsf{s}}}D]
, by assumption and
Lemma

9

(3)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{*}}}[\top\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{*}}}[D\Rightarrow D]]
by axiom T.3

(4)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{*}}}[\top\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{*}}}[C\Rightarrow\top]]
by axiom T.4

(5)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{*}}}[C\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{*}}}[D\Rightarrow D]]
by (3), (4) and rule C.2

(6)

By (1), (2) and by construction, there is a precisification
\pi_{\delta^{\prime},\mathord{\mathop{\lozenge}\nolimits_{\mathsf{s}}}D}\in\pi\in\sigma_{i+1}(\hbox{$\mathsf{s}$})
and by (5) we have
D\in\Lambda_{\pi_{\delta^{\prime},\mathord{\mathop{\lozenge}\nolimits_{\mathsf{s}}}D}}(\delta)
as desired.

Case 2

: Assume
\pi^{\prime}\in\Pi_{i}\setminus\{\pi^{*}\}
,
\pi\in\sigma_{i+1}(\hbox{$\mathsf{s^{\prime}}$})
and
C\in\Lambda_{\pi^{\prime}}(\delta)

(1)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi^{*}}$}}}}[G\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi^{\prime}}$}}}}[\top\Rightarrow C]]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
for some
{G\in\mathrm{Con}(F,\pi^{*})}
by construction and
Lemma

9
.

(2)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi^{\prime}}$}}}}[C\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\lozenge}\nolimits_{\mathsf{s}}}D]
, by assumption

(3)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi^{*}}$}}}}[G\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\lozenge}\nolimits_{\mathsf{s}}}D]
, by (1), (2) and rule F.2

(4)

From (1) and (3), by construction there is a precisification
\pi_{\delta^{\prime},\mathord{\mathop{\lozenge}\nolimits_{\mathsf{s}}}D}\in\pi\in\sigma_{i+1}(\hbox{$\mathsf{s}$})
, and by
Case 1
we have
D\in\Lambda_{\pi_{\delta^{\prime},\mathord{\mathop{\lozenge}\nolimits_{\mathsf{s}}}D}}(\delta)
as desired

Case 3

: Assume
\pi^{\prime}=\pi_{\delta^{\prime},\mathord{\mathop{\lozenge}\nolimits_{\mathsf{s_{1}}}}E}\in\Pi_{i+1}\setminus\Pi_{i}
,
\pi^{\prime}\in\sigma_{i+1}(\hbox{$\mathsf{s}$}^{\prime})
and
C\in\Lambda_{\pi^{\prime}}(\delta)

(1)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi^{*}}$}}}}[G\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi^{\prime}}$}}}}[E\Rightarrow C]]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
for some
{G\in\mathrm{Con}(F,\pi^{*})}

by construction and
Lemma

9

(2)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi^{\prime}}$}}}}[C\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\lozenge}\nolimits_{\mathsf{s}}}D]
, by assumption and
Lemma

9

(3)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi^{*}}$}}}}[H\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\lozenge}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi^{\prime}}$}}}}E]
for some
{H\in\mathrm{Con}(F,\pi^{*})}

by construction and
Lemma

9

(4)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{u_{1}}}}[\top\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi^{*}}$}}}}[F\Rightarrow G]]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
by construction since
G\in\mathrm{Con}(F,\pi^{*})
) and
Lemma

9

(5)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{u_{2}}}}[\top\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi^{*}}$}}}}[F\Rightarrow H]]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
by construction since
H\in\mathrm{Con}(F,\pi^{*})
) and
Lemma

9

(6)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi^{*}}$}}}}[F\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\lozenge}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi^{\prime}}$}}}}E]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
from (5), (3) and rule C.3

(7)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi^{*}}$}}}}[F\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi^{\prime}}$}}}}[E\Rightarrow C]]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
from (4), (1) and rule C.2

(8)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi^{*}}$}}}}[F\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\lozenge}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi^{\prime}}$}}}}C]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
from (6), (7) and rule (C.4)

(9)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi^{*}}$}}}}[F\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\lozenge}\nolimits_{\mathsf{s}}}D]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
from (2), (8) and rule (F.4)

(10)

From (9) by construction there is a precisification
\pi_{\delta^{\prime},\mathord{\mathop{\lozenge}\nolimits_{\mathsf{s}}}D}\in\sigma_{i+1}(\hbox{$\mathsf{s}$})
, and by
Case 1
we have
D\in\Lambda_{\pi_{\delta^{\prime},\mathord{\mathop{\lozenge}\nolimits_{\mathsf{s}}}D}}(\delta)
as desired.

Consequently, from
Cases 1-3
, for all
\pi\in\sigma_{i+1}(\hbox{$\mathsf{s^{\prime}}$})
, if
C\in\Lambda_{\pi}(\delta)
then there is some
\pi_{\delta^{\prime},\mathord{\mathop{\lozenge}\nolimits_{\mathsf{s}}}D}\in\sigma_{i+1}(\hbox{$\mathsf{s}$})
with
D\in\Lambda_{\pi_{\delta^{\prime},\mathord{\mathop{\lozenge}\nolimits_{\mathsf{s}}}D}}(\delta)
, hence
\mathfrak{D}_{0}\models\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{s^{\prime}}}}[C\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\lozenge}\nolimits_{\mathsf{s}}}D]
and by induction also
\mathfrak{D}\models\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{s^{\prime}}}}[C\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\lozenge}\nolimits_{\mathsf{s}}}D]
.

\phi=\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{s}}}{[C\sqsubseteq\exists R.D]}

(notice that
D\neq\mathrm{Self}
)
Let us now show that if
\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{s}}}[C\sqsubseteq\exists R.D]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
then
\mathfrak{D}\models\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{s}}}[C\sqsubseteq\exists R.D]
. That is, we must show that if
C\in\Lambda_{\pi}(\delta)
for some
\pi\in\sigma(\hbox{$\mathsf{s}$})
then there exists
\delta^{\prime}
such that
(\delta,\delta^{\prime})\in R^{\gamma(\pi)}
and
D\in\Lambda_{\pi}(\delta^{\prime})
. Assume that for some
\mathfrak{D}_{i}
we have
C\in\Lambda_{\pi}(\delta)
for
\pi\in\sigma_{i}(\hbox{$\mathsf{s}$})
but there is no
\delta^{\prime}
such that
(\delta,\delta^{\prime})\in R^{\gamma_{i}(\pi)}
and
D\in\Lambda_{\pi}(\delta^{\prime})
. Then for some iteration
k>i
we will pick
\pi=\pi^{*}
and
\delta=\delta^{*}
to produce
\mathfrak{D}_{i+1}
and set
\Delta_{i+1}=\Delta_{i}\cup\{\delta^{\prime}\}
, where
\delta^{\prime}
is a fresh domain element. By the construction,
\Lambda_{\pi}(\delta^{\prime})=\{A\mid\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{u}}}[\top\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{s}}}[D\Rightarrow A]]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash},\hbox{$\mathsf{s}$}\in\sigma^{-1}(\pi^{*})\}
. By axiom T.3 we have
\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{*}}}[\top\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{*}}}[D\Rightarrow D]]
, hence
D\in\Lambda_{\pi}(\delta^{\prime})
as desired. We must now show that also
(\delta,\delta^{\prime})\in R^{\gamma_{i+1}(\pi)}
. This easily follows since
[R^{\gamma_{i+1}(\pi)}]_{0}=\mathsf{Self}\cup\mathsf{Other}
and
(\delta,\delta^{\prime})\in\mathsf{Other}
. Thus
(\delta,\delta^{\prime})\in R^{\gamma_{i+1}(\pi)}
as required.

\phi=\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{s}}}{[\exists R.C\sqsubseteq D]}

Let us show that if
\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{s}}}[\exists R.C\sqsubseteq D]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
then
\mathfrak{D}_{i}\models\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{s}}}[\exists R.C\sqsubseteq D]
. That is, we must show that if
C\in\Lambda_{\pi}(\delta_{2})
and
(\delta_{1},\delta_{2})\in R^{\gamma_{i}(\pi)}
for
\pi\in\sigma_{i}(\hbox{$\mathsf{s}$})
, then
D\in\Lambda_{\pi}(\delta_{1})
.

Remark 1

: If there is a sequence
\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{t_{1}}}}[R_{1}\sqsubseteq R_{2}],\dots,\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{t_{j}}}}[R_{j}\sqsubseteq R_{j+1}]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
for
j\geq 0
, such that
\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{t_{j+1}}}}[R_{j+1}\sqsubseteq R]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
and
\mathsf{t_{1}},\dots,\mathsf{t_{j+1}}\in\sigma^{-1}_{i}(\pi)
, then by
Lemma

9
and the successive application of rule R.1 we have
\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[R_{1}\sqsubseteq R]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}

Base Case

: First, let us show that
\mathfrak{D}_{0}\models\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{s}}}[\exists R.C\sqsubseteq D]
. We let
\pi\in\sigma_{0}(\hbox{$\mathsf{s}$})
,
(\delta_{1},\delta_{2})\in R^{\gamma_{0}(\pi)}
and
C\in\Lambda_{\pi}(\delta_{2})
.

(1)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[\exists R.C\sqsubseteq D]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}
by assumption and
Lemma

9

(2)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{*}}}[\{b\}\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[\top\Rightarrow C]]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}
by construction (since
\delta_{2}=b^{\gamma_{0}}
for some atom
b
) and
Lemma

9

(3)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[R(a,b)]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
for some atom
a
with
\delta_{1}=a^{\gamma_{0}}
by construction and
Lemma

9

(4)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[\{a\}\sqsubseteq\exists R.C]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}
by (2), (3) and rule A.6

(5)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{*}}}[\top\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[\{a\}\Rightarrow D]]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}
by (1), (4) and rule E.3

(6)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[\{a\}\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[\top\Rightarrow D]]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}
by (5),
Lemma

9
and rule A.2, hence
D\in\Lambda_{\pi}(\delta_{1})
as desired

Inductive Step

: Now, we assume that
\mathfrak{D}_{i-1}\models\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{s}}}[\exists R.C\sqsubseteq D]
and show that
\mathfrak{D}_{i}\models\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{s}}}[\exists R.C\sqsubseteq D]
.
So, we need to consider the case where
\delta_{2}
is fresh,
\delta_{2}\in\Delta_{i}\setminus\Delta_{i-1}
. Thus,
\delta_{2}
has been introduced to satisfy some axiom
\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{t}}}[E\sqsubseteq\exists T.F]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
. We must show that if
(\delta_{1},\delta_{2})\in R^{\gamma_{i}(\pi)}
and
C\in\Lambda_{\pi}(\delta_{2})
then
D\in\Lambda_{\pi}(\delta_{1})
for
\pi\in\sigma_{i}(\hbox{$\mathsf{s}$})
. Hence
\delta_{2}=\delta^{\prime}
. First,
(Case Loop)
we consider the -uniquely- self-loop case (where
\delta_{1}=\delta^{\prime}
) for all
\pi\in\Pi
. Then,
(Case Forward)
we assume that
\delta_{1}\neq\delta^{\prime}
, in which case we focus on
\pi=\pi^{*}
since this is the only case that introduces a fresh relation into the saturation process, hence the other cases are trivial. Notice that
(Case Forward)
may contain loops but they do not need to be treated specially.

(Case Loop)

Assume
\delta_{1}=\delta_{2}=\delta^{\prime}
,
C\in\Lambda_{\pi}(\delta^{\prime})
and
(\delta^{\prime},\delta^{\prime})\in R^{\gamma_{i}(\pi)}
.

Case 1

: Assume
\pi=\pi^{*}
.
Since
C\in\Lambda_{\pi}(\delta)
and by
Lemma

9
then
\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{u}}}[\top\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[F\Rightarrow C]]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
.
And since
(\delta^{\prime},\delta^{\prime})\in R^{\gamma_{i}(\pi)}\setminus R^{\gamma_{i-1}(\pi)}
, then there must be
\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{u^{\prime}}}}[\top\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[F\Rightarrow\exists R.\mathsf{Self}]]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
. By application of rules L.2 and E.1, we obtain
\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[F\sqsubseteq\exists R.C]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
. By the premise and
Lemma

9
, we obtain
\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[\exists R.C\sqsubseteq D]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
. Now by rule E.3 we obtain
\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{*}}}[\top\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[F\Rightarrow D]]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
and hence
D\in\Lambda_{\pi}(\delta)
as desired

Case 2

: Assume
\pi\in\Pi_{i}\setminus\{\pi^{*}\}
and
\pi\in\sigma(\hbox{$\mathsf{s}$})
. Since
C\in\Lambda_{\pi}(\delta^{\prime})
then for some
{G,H\in\mathrm{Con}(F,\pi^{*})}
there is
\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi^{*}}$}}}}[G\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[\top\Rightarrow C]]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
by
Lemma

9
. And since
(\delta^{\prime},\delta^{\prime})\in R^{\gamma_{i}(\pi)}\setminus R^{\gamma_{i-1}(\pi)}
, then there must be
\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi^{*}}$}}}}[H\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[\top\Rightarrow\exists R.\mathsf{Self}]]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
.

(1)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[\exists R.C\sqsubseteq D]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
by the assumption

(2)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi^{*}}$}}}}[E\sqsubseteq\exists R.F]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
, by construction and
Lemma

9

(3)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{u_{1}}}}[\top\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi^{*}}$}}}}[F\Rightarrow G]]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}

(4)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{u_{2}}}}[\top\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi^{*}}$}}}}[F\Rightarrow H]]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}

(5)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi^{*}}$}}}}[G\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[\top\Rightarrow C]]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
, by construction and
Lemma

9

(6)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi^{*}}$}}}}[H\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[\top\Rightarrow\exists R.\mathsf{Self}]]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
, by construction and
Lemma

9

(7)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi^{*}}$}}}}[F\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[\top\Rightarrow C]]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
, by (3), (5) and rule C.2

(8)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi^{*}}$}}}}[F\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[\top\Rightarrow\exists R.\mathsf{Self}]]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
, by (4), (6) and rule C.2

(9)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[\exists R.\mathsf{Self}\sqcap C\sqsubseteq D]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
by (1) and rule L.3

(10)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi^{*}}$}}}}[F\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[\top\Rightarrow D]]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
, by (8), (9), (10) and rule E.4, and hence
D\in\Lambda_{\pi}(\delta)
as desired.

Case 3

: Assume
\pi=\pi_{\delta^{\prime},\mathord{\mathop{\lozenge}\nolimits_{\mathsf{s}}}E}
and
\pi\in\sigma(\hbox{$\mathsf{s}$})
. Since
C\in\Lambda_{\pi}(\delta^{\prime})

then for some
{G,H\in\mathrm{Con}(F,\pi^{*})}
by
Lemma

9
there is

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi^{*}}$}}}}[G\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[E\Rightarrow C]]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
and also

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi^{*}}$}}}}[H\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[E\Rightarrow\exists R.\mathsf{Self}]]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}

(1)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[\exists R.C\sqsubseteq D]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
by the assumption

(2)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi^{*}}$}}}}[E\sqsubseteq\exists R.F]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
, by construction and
Lemma

9

(3)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{u_{1}}}}[\top\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi^{*}}$}}}}[F\Rightarrow G]]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}

(4)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{u_{2}}}}[\top\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi^{*}}$}}}}[F\Rightarrow H]]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}

(5)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi^{*}}$}}}}[E\sqsubseteq\exists R.F]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
, by construction and
Lemma

9

(6)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi^{*}}$}}}}[G\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[E\Rightarrow C]]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
, by construction and
Lemma

9

(7)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi^{*}}$}}}}[H\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[E\Rightarrow\exists R.\mathsf{Self}]]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
, by construction and
Lemma

9

(8)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi^{*}}$}}}}[F\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[E\Rightarrow C]]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
, by (3), (6) and rule C.2

(9)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi^{*}}$}}}}[F\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[E\Rightarrow\exists R.\mathsf{Self}]]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
, by (4), (7) and rule C.2

(10)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[\exists R.\mathsf{Self}\sqcap C\sqsubseteq D]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
by (1) and rule L.3

(11)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi^{*}}$}}}}[F\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[E\Rightarrow D]]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
, by (8), (9), (10) and rule E.4, and hence
D\in\Lambda_{\pi}(\delta^{\prime})
as desired

(Case Forward)

Now, since we have
(\delta_{1},\delta^{\prime})\in R^{\gamma_{i+1}(\pi)}
then by the saturation process that constructs
[R^{\gamma_{i+1}(\pi)}]_{k}
there must be a sequence of domain elements
\delta^{\prime}_{1},\dots,\delta^{\prime}_{m},\delta^{\prime}_{m+1}
with
\delta^{\prime}_{1}=\delta_{1}
and
\delta^{\prime}_{m}=\delta
.
We notice that by construction we have the following formulas:

(P1)

We have
C=C^{\prime}_{m+1}
,
R=R^{\prime}_{{1|m}}
and
T=R_{{m|m}}

(P2)

C_{j},C^{\prime}_{j}\in\Lambda_{\pi}(\delta^{\prime}_{j})
for all
j\in\{k,\ldots,m\}

(P3)

There is some
k\in\{1,\ldots,m-1\}
such that

(P3.a)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[C^{\prime}_{j}\sqsubseteq\exists R_{j|j}.C_{j+1}]\in\mathcal{K}^{\vdash}
for all
j\in\{k,\ldots,m\}
, and

(P3.b)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[R_{j|j}(a_{j},a_{j+1})]\in\mathcal{K}^{\vdash}
for all
j\in\{1,\ldots,k-1\}

(P4)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[R_{j|j}\sqsubseteq R^{\prime}_{j|j}],\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[R_{j|m}\sqsubseteq R^{\prime}_{j|m}]\in\mathcal{K}^{\vdash}
(Notice that by axiom T.5 and
Lemma

9
we can obtain formulas
\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[R^{\prime}\sqsubseteq R^{\prime}]\in\mathcal{K}^{\vdash}
)

(P5)

If
1\leq j<m
,
\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[R^{\prime}_{{j|j}}\circ R^{\prime}_{j+1|m}\sqsubseteq R_{j|m}]\in\mathcal{K}^{\vdash}

We first show that we can obtain
\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[C^{\prime}_{m}\sqsubseteq\exists R^{\prime}_{m|m}.C^{\prime}_{m+1}]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
:

(1)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{u}}}[\top\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[C_{m+1}\Rightarrow C^{\prime}_{m+1}]]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
from
C\in\Lambda_{\pi}(\delta^{\prime}_{m+1})
by construction with
C=C^{\prime}_{m+1}

(2)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[C^{\prime}_{m}\sqsubseteq\exists R_{m|m}.C_{m+1}]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
by the assumption of
Case 1

(3)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[R_{m|m}\sqsubseteq R^{\prime}_{m|m}]\in\mathcal{K}^{\vdash}
by construction (P4)

(4)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[C^{\prime}_{m}\sqsubseteq\exists R^{\prime}_{m|m}.C^{\prime}_{m+1}]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
by (1), (2), (3) and rule E.1

We show that for
j>k
, if we have
\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[C^{\prime}_{j}\sqsubseteq\exists R^{\prime}_{j|m}.C^{\prime}_{m+1}]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
then we can obtain
\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[C^{\prime}_{j-1}\sqsubseteq\exists R^{\prime}_{{j-1}|m}.C^{\prime}_{m+1}]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
.

(1)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{u}}}[\top\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[C_{j}\Rightarrow C^{\prime}_{j}]]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
from
C^{\prime}_{j}\in\Lambda_{\pi}(\delta^{\prime}_{j})
by construction

(2)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[C^{\prime}_{j-1}\sqsubseteq\exists R_{{j-1}|{j-1}}.C_{j}]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
by construction (P3.a)

(3)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[R_{{j-1}|{j-1}}\sqsubseteq R^{\prime}_{{j-1}|{j-1}}]\in\mathcal{K}^{\vdash}
by construction (P4)

(4)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[R^{\prime}_{{{j-1}|{j-1}}}\circ R^{\prime}_{j|m}\sqsubseteq R_{{j-1}|m}]\in\mathcal{K}^{\vdash}
by construction (P5)

(5)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[C^{\prime}_{j}\sqsubseteq\exists R^{\prime}_{j|m}.C^{\prime}_{m+1}]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
by inductive hypothesis

(6)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[C^{\prime}_{j-1}\sqsubseteq\exists R^{\prime}_{{j-1}|{j-1}}.C^{\prime}_{j}]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
by (1), (2), (3) and rule E.1

(7)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[C^{\prime}_{j-1}\sqsubseteq\exists R^{\prime}_{{j-1}|m}.C^{\prime}_{m+1}]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
by (4), (5), (6) and rule E.2

Now, assume
k=1
; we show the base case where
j=1
.

(1)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[\exists R^{\prime}_{{1|m}}.C^{\prime}_{m+1}\sqsubseteq D]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
by the assumption

(2)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[C^{\prime}_{1}\sqsubseteq\exists R^{\prime}_{{1|m}}.C^{\prime}_{m+1}]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
by the inductive hypothesis

(3)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{u}}}[\top\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[F\Rightarrow C^{\prime}_{1}]]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
from
C^{\prime}_{1}\in\Lambda_{\pi}(\delta^{\prime})
by construction

(4)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{*}}}[\top\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[C^{\prime}_{1}\Rightarrow D]]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
by (1), (2) and rule E.3

(5)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{u}}}[\top\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[C^{\prime}_{1}\Rightarrow D]]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
by (4), and rule S.3

(6)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{u}}}[\top\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[F\Rightarrow D]]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
by (3), (5) and rule C.1 and hence
D\in\Lambda_{\pi}(\delta_{1})
as desired

Else, in case
j=k\neq 1
, we first we obtain

(1)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[R^{\prime}_{j-1|j-1}(a_{j-1},a_{j})]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
by assumption (P3.b)

(2)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[C^{\prime}_{j}\sqsubseteq\exists R^{\prime}_{{j}|m}.C^{\prime}_{m+1}]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
by assumption

(3)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{*}}}[\{a_{j}\}\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[\top\Rightarrow C^{\prime}_{j}]]\in\mathcal{K}^{\vdash}

(4)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[R^{\prime}_{{j-1|j-1}}\circ R^{\prime}_{j|m}\sqsubseteq R_{j|m}]\in\mathcal{K}^{\vdash}
by assumption (P5)

(5)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[R_{{j-1}|{m}}\sqsubseteq R^{\prime}_{{j-1}|{m}}]\in\mathcal{K}^{\vdash}
by construction (P4)

(6)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{*}}}[\{a_{j-1}\}\sqsubseteq\exists R_{{j-1}|m}.C^{\prime}_{m+1}]\in\mathcal{K}^{\vdash}
by (1), (2), (3), (4) and rule A.8

(7)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{*}}}[\{a_{j-1}\}\sqsubseteq\exists R^{\prime}_{{j-1}|m}.C^{\prime}_{m+1}]\in\mathcal{K}^{\vdash}
by (5), (6) and rule A.4

Else, in case
j\in\{2,\ldots,k-1\}
if we have
\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{*}}}[\{a_{j}\}\sqsubseteq\exists R^{\prime}_{{j}|m}.C^{\prime}_{m+1}]\in\mathcal{K}^{\vdash}
then we can obtain
\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{*}}}[\{a_{j-1}\}\sqsubseteq\exists R^{\prime}_{{j-1}|m}.C^{\prime}_{m+1}]\in\mathcal{K}^{\vdash}
.

(1)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[R_{j-1|j-1}(a_{j-1},a_{j})]
by construction (P3.b)

(2)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{*}}}[\{a_{j}\}\sqsubseteq\exists R^{\prime}_{{j}|m}.C^{\prime}_{m+1}]\in\mathcal{K}^{\vdash}
by assumption

(3)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[R^{\prime}_{{j-1|j-1}}\circ R^{\prime}_{j|m}\sqsubseteq R_{j|m}]\in\mathcal{K}^{\vdash}
by assumption (P5)

(4)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[R_{{j-1}|{m}}\sqsubseteq R^{\prime}_{{j-1}|{m}}]\in\mathcal{K}^{\vdash}
by construction (P4)

(5)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{*}}}[\{a_{j-1}\}\sqsubseteq\exists R_{{j-1}|m}.C^{\prime}_{m+1}]\in\mathcal{K}^{\vdash}
by (1), (2), (3) and rule A.7

(6)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{*}}}[\{a_{j-1}\}\sqsubseteq\exists R^{\prime}_{{j-1}|m}.C^{\prime}_{m+1}]\in\mathcal{K}^{\vdash}
by (4), (5) and rule A.4

Finally, assume the base case where
j=1
and
k\neq 1

(1)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[\exists R^{\prime}_{{1|m}}.C^{\prime}_{m+1}\sqsubseteq D]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
by the assumption

(2)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{*}}}[\{a_{1}\}\sqsubseteq\exists R^{\prime}_{{1}|m}.C^{\prime}_{m+1}]\in\mathcal{K}^{\vdash}
by induction

(3)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{u}}}[\top\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[\{a_{1}\}\Rightarrow D]]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
by (1), (2) and rule E.3

(4)

\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{u}}}[\{a_{1}\}\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[\top\Rightarrow D]]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
by (3) and rule I.1,
D\in\Lambda_{\pi}(\delta_{1})
as desired

\phi=\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{s}}}(C(a))\

Assume
\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{s}}}(C(a))\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}
(i.e.
\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{*}}}[\{a\}\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{s}}}[\top\Rightarrow C]]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
).
Then, by construction
\delta_{a}=a^{\gamma_{0}}
and
C\in\Lambda_{\pi}(\delta_{a})
.

\phi=\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{s}}}(R(a,b))\

Assume
\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{s}}}(R(a,b))\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}
.
Then, by construction
\delta_{a}=a^{\gamma_{0}}
,
\delta_{b}=b^{\gamma_{0}}
and
(\delta_{a},\delta_{b})\in R^{\gamma_{0}(\pi)}
for all
\pi\in\sigma_{0}(\hbox{$\mathsf{s}$})
. Thus also by construction
\delta_{a}=a^{\gamma}
,
\delta_{b}=b^{\gamma}
and
(\delta_{a},\delta_{b})\in R^{\gamma(\pi)}
for all
\pi\in\sigma(\hbox{$\mathsf{s}$})
and hence
\mathfrak{D}\models\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{s}}}(R(a,b))
.

With this, Claim
7
is proved.

Now, returning to the proof of the completeness theorem, we show that it follows from the claim above. It remains to show that
\mathfrak{D}
is indeed a model. First, we observe that clearly, by the construction of
\Pi_{0}
and
\sigma_{0}
, no standpoint is empty. Moreover, by axiom
T.1
we obtain that
\sigma_{0}(*)=\Pi_{0}
as required, and we remark that by construction this carries to
\sigma(*)=\Pi
. Finally, we need to make sure that for all
\pi\in\Pi
and
\delta\in\Delta
,
\bot\notin\Lambda_{\pi}(\delta)
, so all domain elements can be instantiated in all precisisfications.

Assume for the sake of contradiction that
\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{*}}}[\top\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{*}}}[\top\Rightarrow\bot]]\notin\mathcal{K}^{\vdash}
but in
\mathfrak{D}
there is
\bot\in\Lambda_{\pi}(\delta^{\prime})
for some
\delta^{\prime}\in\Delta
. First, assume that
\delta^{\prime}\in\Delta_{0}
of
\mathfrak{D}_{0}
, and thus
\bot\in\Lambda_{\pi}(\delta^{\prime})
. Then by construction there is some
\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{u}}}[\{a\}\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[\top\Rightarrow\bot]]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
for some named individual
a
. But then by rule B.4 we have
\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{*}}}[\top\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{*}}}[\top\Rightarrow\bot]]\in\mathcal{K}^{\vdash}
, thus reaching a contradiction. Thus, if
\delta^{\prime}\notin\Delta_{0}
of
\mathfrak{D}_{0}
, there must be a
\mathfrak{D}_{i+1}
with
\delta^{\prime}\in\Delta_{i+1}\setminus\Delta_{i}
and
\bot\in\Lambda_{\pi}(\delta^{\prime})
. Notice that the iteration has been triggered at
\pi^{*}
to satisfy an axiom of the form
\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{t}}}[E\sqsubseteq\exists R.F]
, and by
Lemma

9
we have
\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi^{*}}$}}}}[E\sqsubseteq\exists R.F]
.

Case
\pi=\pi^{*}

By construction we have
\Lambda_{\pi^{*}}(\delta^{\prime})=\{A\mid\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{u}}}[\top\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{s}}}[F\Rightarrow A]]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash},\hbox{$\mathsf{s}$}\in\sigma^{-1}(\pi^{*})\}
and thus by the assumption we have some
\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{u}}}[\top\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi^{*}}$}}}}[F\Rightarrow\bot]]
. By axiom T.5 and
Lemma

9
we obtain also
\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi^{*}}$}}}}[R\sqsubseteq R]
and by rule E.1 we get
\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi^{*}}$}}}}[E\sqsubseteq\exists R.\bot]
. Then, by rule B.1 we obtain
\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{*}}}[\top\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi^{*}}$}}}}[E\Rightarrow\bot]]
. This means that in
\mathfrak{D}_{i}
there is
\bot\in\Lambda_{\pi^{*}}(\delta^{*})
since
\delta^{*}\in\Delta_{i}
.

Case
\pi\in\Pi_{i}\setminus\pi^{*}

By construction and
Lemma

9
if
\bot\in\Lambda_{\pi}(\delta^{\prime})
then
\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi^{*}}$}}}}[G\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[\top\Rightarrow\bot]]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
for
G\in\Lambda_{\pi^{*}}(\delta^{\prime})
. By rule B.2 we obtain
\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{*}}}[\top\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi^{*}}$}}}}[G\Rightarrow\bot]]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
, which leads to
\bot\in\Lambda_{\pi^{*}}(\delta^{\prime})
and, by the previous case (
Case
\pi=\pi^{*}
), in
\mathfrak{D}_{i}
there is
\bot\in\Lambda_{\pi^{*}}(\delta^{*})
since
\delta^{*}\in\Delta_{i}
.

Case
\pi=\pi_{\delta^{\prime},\mathord{\mathop{\lozenge}\nolimits_{\mathsf{s}}}E}

By construction and
Lemma

9
if
\bot\in\Lambda_{\pi_{\delta^{\prime},\mathord{\mathop{\lozenge}\nolimits_{\mathsf{s}}}E}}(\delta^{\prime})
then
\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi^{*}}$}}}}[G\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[E\Rightarrow\bot]]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
and
\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi^{*}}$}}}}[H\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\lozenge}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}E]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
for
G,H\in\Lambda_{\pi^{*}}(\delta^{\prime})
. By rule C.2 we obtain that also
\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi^{*}}$}}}}[F\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}[E\Rightarrow\bot]]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
and by rule C.3 we obtain
\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi^{*}}$}}}}[F\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\lozenge}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}E]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
. Then by rule C.4 we obtain
\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi^{*}}$}}}}[F\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\lozenge}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi}$}}}}\bot]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
and by rule B.3 we get
\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{*}}}[\top\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{\hbox{$\mathsf{s}_{\pi^{*}}$}}}}[F\Rightarrow\bot]]\in\text{$\mathcal{K}$}^{\vdash}
. Hence, by the first case (
Case
\pi=\pi^{*}
) in
\mathfrak{D}_{i}
there is
\bot\in\Lambda_{\pi^{*}}(\delta^{*})
since
\delta^{*}\in\Delta_{i}
.

Thus, if there is some
\delta\in\Delta_{i}
of
\mathfrak{D}_{i}
such that
\bot\in\Lambda_{\pi}(\delta)
, then there is
\delta^{\prime}\in\Delta_{0}
of
\mathfrak{D}_{0}
such that
\bot\in\Lambda_{\pi}(\delta^{\prime})
, but as we showed this implies
\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{*}}}[\top\sqsubseteq\mathord{\mathop{\square}\nolimits_{\mathsf{*}}}[\top\Rightarrow\bot]]\in\mathcal{K}^{\vdash}
, thus reaching a contradiction.
